This paper introduces two methods to analyze how input units interact to influence neural network outputs, focusing on local contextual effects. The approach was successfully applied to explain AlphaGo Zero's gaming strategies.
-----
-----
2894
This paper introduces a Bayesian model for inferring contrastive temporal logic specifications that highlight differences between two sets of plan traces, demonstrating its effectiveness across multiple planning domains and a simulated air combat scenario.
